## Root-cause analysis and correction

This attempt is **FAIL / HW UNVERIFIED** and did not complete the CH1 CC point. The
confirmed CH1 CV point was recorded above, but it belongs to the pre-correction
source and must be replayed with the rest of H03. For CH1 CC, the wire trace shows:

- `CHAN:OUTP 1` was sent through the driver and the instrument replied to
  `CHAN:OUTP?` with `1`.
- The next `STAT:OPER:INST:ISUM1/2/3:COND?` replies were `0`, causing the old
  `output_states()` implementation to report every channel OFF.
- Every observed `SYST:ERR?` response was `0, "No error"`. The fail-safe turned
  PSU and KEL103 INPUT OFF; the operator then confirmed all three outputs and
  KEL103 INPUT OFF.
- The runner stopped before reading voltage/current/power or asking for a CC
  display confirmation. No electrical CC result is claimed for this attempt.

The cause was using the operation-condition bit 3 as the sole ON/OFF readback.
The [official Keithley 2231A reference manual](https://download.tek.com/manual/077100401_Referencemanual.pdf)
defines `CHAN:OUTP?` as the 0/1 query for the selected channel and documents bit 3
of the operation instrument summary separately as ON. The physical response in
this attempt proves those sources disagreed for this state; it does not establish
why the firmware status register returned zero.

The library now reads `INST:NSEL?`, selects each channel, obtains `CHAN:OUTP?`,
and restores the actual selection. Profile and emergency OFF checks share this
path. The H03 runner treats absent CV/CC mode bits as unqualified rather than
silently inferring a mode; it continues only after the operator confirms the PSU
display and the independent KEL103 readings agree. An explicit conflicting mode
bit still stops the point. `read_channel()` preserves `UNKNOWN` when both mode
registers omit their mode bits.

Regression evidence: the new output-state test first failed against the old
implementation, then passed with the direct channel query. A second regression
ensures the actual panel channel selection is restored. A simulated H03 scenario
with both status mode registers empty completes all six measured points as
`PARTIAL` without inventing CV/CC. The full offline suite passed **88/88** on the
corrected source (`PYTHONPATH=src:tests:tests/hardware python3 -m unittest discover -s tests`).
This is SIM TESTED only. A full physical H03 replay is still required.
